HP 60 (KatAM's bosses' for one player; Squeak Squad's 300, her crash's 20 and last stand's 20 scaled to 4). Her things (`SQUEAK_MOLEY_THING`, Squeak Squad's speeds per kind, bouncing, 240 frames, a bolt 380; bursting on Kirby or a hit; swallowed, a ring gives Wheel, a bomb Bomb, a can Fire, a saw Cutter) and dirt (`SQUEAK_MOLEY_DIRT`: the petals' burst, petals, her shadow). Drawn from `mrsMoley_cmn`, `_ext00` and `_ext01` merged (their palettes the same, shared), both `.mca`'s composites (`KeepComposites`). Checked against DS recordings in her own stage and in KatAM (USA, room 0x323). Her sounds: Squeak Squad's (`SqueakSfx`). Not ported: her own defeat (KatAM's), Squeak Squad's hit flash, the things' 2-frame bounce shakes, her boss music. |
| Yadgaine | Squeak Squad's boss 0x3B | `squeak/yadgaine.c` | A boss on the mid-bosses' framework (mini-boss type 0x40, the template's `subtype1` 0xAA), a robot in a spiked shell chasing Kirby with its two legs, at three quarters of the DS's size (the owner's pick: drawn pixel for pixel, its body and shell alike; the DS's numbers below, in KatAM its boxes, its spots, how far ahead its things start, its moves' own speed and its knocked-up blocks' flight three quarters of them: `SIZED`, each move as long as the DS's; what it throws, the rocks and the scroll as they are). Squeak Squad's stage scrolls for good (the camera's lock moved right every frame, the stage 512000 pixels wide); KatAM's rooms don't, so the screen stays and the scroll is taken out of everything of its fight: Yadgaine and its things move at their speed less the scroll's, the Kirbys in its room are carried back at the scroll's speed, and its front is a wall (nothing goes behind it: a Kirby there is pushed to it). Its spots are from the camera's view at the fight's start: its own from the view's left (64 and 110 pixels in; held between the view's left and 110 in), the rocks' over the view's right. Daroach comes down over it (80 up: his animations 1-5, then off up and back at 2.0); 120 frames, hidden; the scroll at 1.0 150 frames; it shows and rushes in at 2.0 from the view's left to 64 in. Each round (the scroll and it at 0.5, 90 frames) rocks fall every 15 frames (of 100: under 20 a small one, 28 a big one, 30 a gordo, over the view's right 120 pixels, 80 when it's forward; at its speed, 0.1 gravity; rocks break on the ground, a gordo bounces once, 2.5 up, and falls through), then it picks (of 100): with a leg left, back (64 in) under 20 its swipe, 40 its lunge, 60 its shot, else its dash; forward (110 in) under 45 its swipe, 90 back, else a 20-frame pause; with none left, before its second form back under 70 its shot, else its dash; forward under 70 back, else the short pause; in its second form, back under 70 its dash, forward under 70 back, else a 50-frame pause. Dash: the scroll 1.0, it 1.3, till 110 in; back: the scroll 1.0, it 0.8, till 64 in; swipe: legs raised 30 frames, struck down 120; lunge: the scroll 1.0, legs raised 30, struck down 120 while it goes 1.5; shot: a puff 5 ahead, 10 up, 20 frames on its capsule (21 ahead, 3.5 forward, through everything), 80 frames. Its legs (`SQUEAK_YADGAINE_LEG`, the back one behind it, the front one in front) follow it: walking, raised, struck and resting; each time a resting foot touches the ground (`yadgaine.rca`'s 0x201 box), of 100, under 4, 7 or 12 a block (`SQUEAK_YADGAINE_THING` 2-4: 2.0 or 3.0 forward, 4.0, 5.0 or 3.0 up) flies off it, landing as a solid tile of KatAM's (its 16-pixel cell, stepping a cell back for each 16 pixels the screen would scroll), broken when it's under Yadgaine (32 ahead of the leg), by a hit or swallowed. A hit on its body takes the same damage off both legs; a leg's own hits are its own (its HP kept over 100 for KatAM, which would beat it at 0); at 0 a leg stays broken, hurting by touch, untouchable (level 6). Its body 20 frames safe after a hit (KatAM's damage undone); 72 damage on it (Squeak Squad's 360 of 400) and its second form: its cracked body (`.mca`'s 1), a new round. Kirby's body doesn't hurt the legs and things, nor are they swallowed (KatAM's touch of a flashing Kirby, and its helpers' inhales, beat them outright). HP 80 (Squeak Squad's 400, as Mrs. Moley's 300 is 60), its back leg 58 (290), its front one 44 (220). Its name's "Y" is drawn (no KatAM name has one: `HandLetter`). Checked against DS recordings in its own stage and in KatAM (USA, room 0x323). Its shell, which Squeak Squad draws on a background layer from its stage's tiles (154 by 208 pixels, 312 tiles; KatAM's background tiles and palettes are all taken in a fight), is a sprite at three quarters of its size as its body (`KatAMExpansionSqueak.cs`'s `SqueakShell` and `SqueakShrink`, as its body's frames: only what the fight ever shows; each of the DS's pixels goes to the pixel its middle lands in, an outline's winning, so outlines stay one pixel wide and unbroken, and a stair's doubled corner is taken out (taking every pixel an outline crossed a quarter of drew them one and two pixels thick, in jagged stairs); 174 tiles; at the DS's size it was 288, and stored smaller and drawn bigger by the GBA's sprite scaling it was too coarse beside its body), where the DS has it (bobbing with its body), behind it, its legs and the room's foreground (OAM priority 3), till it's beaten (as its legs); its tiles and colors are loaded once and its pieces written into KatAM's OAM buffer together by hand each frame (KatAM's slot allocator, a call a piece, isn't used), those farthest from its body first (on a line with more sprites than the GBA has time for, the last ones aren't drawn: behind its body that doesn't show), its palette Daroach's slot once he's gone (KatAM's 7 enemy palettes are all taken in this fight). The fight was near the GBA's limits before it: KatAM's sprite heap (512 tiles in 2-tile segments) reaches 428 when two of its rocks' bursts (64 tiles each) are out, so Squeak Squad's effects now wait for room (`SQUEAK_FX_ROOM`: an effect is left out when it would leave under 48 tiles for KatAM's own, which draw whatever is in VRAM when they get none), and a character whose type's VRAM can't be got asks for its own size instead. Measured in the USA build (room 0x323, 1,500 frames, Kirby idle with helpers): 86 lag frames, against 131 with the scaled shell before and 133 without a shell (its knocked-up blocks at the DS's flight beside its smaller spots piled up, 271: their flight is shrunk with it); no sprite drawn from bad VRAM, the 7 palettes holding; 9 or 10 rock bursts shown (about 11 without a shell, 4 with the scaled one); the GBA's sprite time per line (1,210 cycles) never passed, 1,016 at worst. Not ported: the floor crumbling behind it, the screen scrolling, its sounds, the DS's camera jolts as it walks, steps and throws, its hit flash, Squeak Squad's lesser damage from some attacks, its own defeat (KatAM's), its walk-off when Kirby is beaten, its boss music. |
| Bohboh | Squeak Squad's boss 0x3C | `squeak/bohboh.c` | A boss on the mid-bosses' framework (mini-boss type 0x40, the template's `subtype1` 0xAF), a fiery bird over a lava pool. Its arena is from the camera's view middle across and the floor where it was placed (Squeak Squad's: the lava 128 pixels across the middle at the floor, its points 84 right and 72 left of the middle and 96 over the floor, a third in the lava; its zones left of the lava, over it, right of it). Its intro: far off and small in the background, 236 left of the middle and 100 up, it waits 60 frames, crosses the view at 2.0 and comes in big from 80 out and 80 over the right point to it (60 frames); it brings in the lava's hurt (an object of its own over the lava: 128 by 10, hurting what touches it; the stage's lava isn't in KatAM's rooms, so it shows nothing). It waits 30 or 60 frames (50 in 100), then picks (of 100, with fewer than two of its dropped enemies out: under 20 its leap, 30 its charge, 40 its pass, 60 its swoop, else it waits; with more: 50, 70, 90, else it waits). Leap: to the other point, 3.0 across, 3.5 down, 0.11 up a frame (no more down or up out of view), turned round over the other zone, settling in 8 frames. Charge: at its height at 2.0 past the other point and back past its own, a fire every 30 frames. Pass: on at 2.0 out of view with its fires, back far off in the background, in big to that side's point. After either, with fewer than two enemies out, its swoop 30 in 100. Swoop: backing up 60 frames (0.4 back, 0.2 up), then at Kirby's feet at 4.0; out of view it comes in big to its side's point; it grabs a Kirby its claws touch, carries him 22 pixels under it to the other point (60 frames) and throws him down at 5.0 toward the lava's middle, letting him go hurt in the lava (it can't be hit while it holds him). Into the lava, it dives (0.2 back, 2.0 up, 0.2 gravity), the lava taking 4 of its HP, and sits in it 120 frames (hit there by its own box); it can be hit anywhere but far off in the background and in its dive (its boxes flagged untouchable there), nor while it holds Kirby, 32 frames safe after a hit; every 40 + rand(20) frames a fire (0.1 gravity, bursting on the ground) or, 30 in 100, one of the stage's enemies (KatAM's Waddle Dee, Pengy, Sir Kibble or Hot Head, one of each at most) falls from a spot across the view's top; then it rises at 0.5 to its side's point, turns to the middle and flares (20 frames). HP 70 (Squeak Squad's 350, as Mrs. Moley's 300 is 60). Checked against DS recordings in its own stage and in KatAM (USA, room 0x323). Not ported: the lava's look (the stage's tiles), its sounds, the camera's shakes, its hit flash, its own defeat (KatAM's), its boss music. |
| Daroach, Dark Daroach | Squeak Squad's boss 0x3D (its parameter 0, a6s7s2's; 1, a8s4s2's: Dark Daroach) | `squeak/daroach_boss.c` | Bosses on the mid-bosses' framework (mini-boss type 0x40, the template's `subtype1` 0xB1 and 0xB3), drawn from `dancho00` and `dancho01` (the sprite his body switches to for his wand) in one palette set: dancho00's colors, Dark Daroach's dancho01's (`SharePalettes`, `PaletteFile`). His arena is from the camera's view middle across and the floor where he was placed (a6s7s2's: points 80 left and right of the middle, 92 and 20 over the floor, and one 8 right of it 64 up, where he starts; Kirby's zones left, middle and right). He floats his intro, then idles at his point (facing in; at the middle point toward Kirby), 10 or 40 frames, and picks by Kirby's zone: off Kirby's side a wait of 60 more (1 in 9), a warp (3 in 9: to Kirby's side, or three warps across and between the low and top points), his stars or a bomb; on it a warp to the far low point or a hop across (3 in 9), else his stars or his beam. Warp: he vanishes, shows at the point 10 frames later (untouchable meanwhile). Hop: to the point in 40, 30 or 50 frames by distance, 0.1 gravity. Beam: a hop to his point's x at Kirby's height, his wand up, its charge 60 frames following Kirby's height (0.05 of it a frame, 2.5 at most), then the beam 120 frames, 31 to 332 pixels ahead (Dark Daroach's 31 to 320, taller, backing off at 0.3): its reach two objects of their own (KatAM's boxes reach 127 pixels), its 16 parts drawn past 127 pixels (`SqueakPartFx`'s offsets now 16-bit). Bomb: lobbed at Kirby (45 frames, 0.05 gravity), bursting on the ground, a Bomb when inhaled; Dark Daroach's lands, 180 frames later lights and burns a column 128 up 120 frames. Stars: three at 4.0 (45°, 75° and 15° from straight down), bouncing off the terrain at one of the three angles, gone after 100 frames or on touching Kirby; inhaled for a star to spit. Hit anywhere but in his warp (his boxes flagged there), 10 frames safe after. HP 80 (Squeak Squad's 400). Checked against DS recordings in his stage (his moves, his box test per state) and in KatAM (USA, room 0x323: both). His sounds: Squeak Squad's (`SqueakSfx`). Not ported: the screen's shakes, his hit flash, his own defeat (his wand bouncing off: KatAM's), his boss music. |
| Dark Nebula | Squeak Squad's bosses 0x46 (his coming, a8s4s5) and 0x3F (his fight, a8s4s6) | `squeak/dark_nebula.c` | A boss on the mid-bosses' framework (mini-boss type 0x40, the template's `subtype1` 0xBA), Squeak Squad's last: his coming and his fight in one room. Drawn from `darkZERO_cmn` and `darkZERO_ext` in one palette set; his eye and his flames are sprites of their own over him (Squeak Squad's +0x1F8 and +0x1FC), each of his things a character of its own (twinkle, aura, stars, fireball, fire, ice pillar, bolts and cage, the burning floor). His arena is from the camera's view middle across and the floor where he was placed (Squeak Squad's spots are from its 256 by 192 view: a spot `a` across is a - 128 from the middle, `b` down is 144 - b over the floor). His coming: a dark star flies in from 360 pixels left at 3.0 (120 frames), spins, forms, his eye opens, looks about three times and glints. His fight: the black star 50 frames, a shake, his eye's twinkle, the screen whitens 60 frames, a form at random shows (fire, spark or ice), the screen clears 60 frames; then 40 frames idle (his eye half shut) and a pick by form: fire 35 in 100 his dashes (3-5 at 5.0 between seven spots), 25 his fireball (to the top middle, 40 frames, then it falls; 10 HP, broken it throws 5 sparks; on the floor a burst, its fire wall 50-69 frames, and flames either way at 2.6 that set each 16-pixel tile of the floor they pass burning 47 frames, hurting); spark 30 his wave (2-3 waves across at 1.0), 40 his bolts (to the middle, four bolts to the view's corners, then a cage whose sides, floor and top hurt, 40 frames); ice 30 his orbit (1-2 laps of an ellipse, speeding up), 40 his ice pillar (from a top corner, he and the pillar under him drift across at 0.8); else (40 or 30 in 100) a shared move: a row of stars across from a low corner, a rain of stars at Kirby from a top corner, four stars from the top middle, or a drop of five stars as he crosses the top at 2.0. In the moves where his eye shoots, each frame 1 in 100 a star at 1.5 ahead of Kirby, else 4 in 100 one at Kirby at 2.0. After 3-5 moves he picks one of the other two forms; 240 frames later, whatever he's doing, he swoops to the top middle (20 frames), waits 30, twinkles, and the screen whitens to his new form. Hit only in a form and not while the screen whitens or clears, 60 frames blinking (safe) after a hit; his touch hurts in a form. HP 60 (Squeak Squad's 300). Checked against the DS (his stage recorded; each move forced by a RAM write in the DS and in KatAM from the same spot, his spots, states and things frame by frame, screenshots side by side) and in KatAM (USA, room 0x323: every move, the form change, an unforced fight of 2400 frames with helpers; his defeat, HP written down to 2 by a RAM write, then a helper's hit: KatAM's mini-boss defeat, his black star falling at the arena's side, the explosion, gone). His defeat is the DS's (0x0211DF2C-0x0211CDCC): KatAM's mini-boss defeat is caught the frame it starts (his `unk7C`, `DarkNebulaWatch()`: its writes undone, his HP left at 0) and his runs: the controls taken, the far view darkening to black over 240 frames, three whitenings and clearings with his forms flickering (and his black star), his black star at the view's middle, the far view back, the controls given back, his eye shaking, sparks thrown off him (Squeak Squad effects that fall and bounce once on the floor: `SqueakFallInRoom()`), his star shaking, his music fading, his star breaking up, his eye left alone, its twinkle, the white-out; then the white clears and he's gone (the finale's dance-out). In his own room (8-4-6) his floor is the one under his spot (the dark star's, in the air). Checked in KatAM (USA, Europe, Japan, room 0x5E7, KatAM's defeat's writes made by hand: the DS's states, timings and pictures). His form's tint (the DS's 0x021225B4 and 0x021222E4): the far view's colors go toward his form's own over 120 frames, at his fight's start and at each form he picks (fire: red kept, green and blue halved; spark: green; ice: blue), and stay so through KatAM's palette effects (the far view's own colors kept by a small task of his, `FarViewKept()`, the tinted ones written where KatAM's flashes copy back from), back to its own at his defeat. His HP bar shows from his black star's wait's end with its sound (0x020B308C, 0x177), not from his coming (KatAM's HUD brings it in about 55 frames later and fills it). His music is his rooms' own (map music 37), which his fight never changes, as on the DS. Checked in KatAM (USA; the tint also in Europe and Japan): the far view's colors logged and seen through his forms, an ability flash and his defeat; the song unchanged from his room's start to his defeat. His sounds: Squeak Squad's (`SqueakSfx`). Not ported: the screen shakes' strength (KatAM's), stars turned back by Squeak Squad's reflecting attacks (KatAM has none), the fire's heat (harmless on the DS), the stage's +0x9C bit 2 at his form changes (it only holds a few of the DS Kirby's own states), his defeat's message at the white-out (0x020217D0: an effect's animation set while the screen is all white); the floor's burning time is the DS's average (42-52 frames there). |
| Dark star | Squeak Squad's 0x3E (a8s4s3 and a8s4s4, before Dark Nebula) | `squeak/dark_star.c` | The dark star Kirby chases to Dark Nebula (`darkZERO_cmn`'s animation 1; its .rca has only a body box: it hurts nobody, nothing hits it and it can't be inhaled). It faces away from Kirby as it appears and moves backward: toward Kirby's side, past him and on, leading him (on the DS it starts behind him at the stage's left end). Start: 1.2 a frame for 120 frames, 0.03 gravity, on the terrain, bouncing on floors (3.5 up, 0.15 gravity from then on). Then hops through everything: 3.5 up, 0.15 gravity, across at Kirby's speed + 0.88, in place while it's more than 64 pixels ahead of him; a new hop once it falls as fast as it rose, comes within 12 pixels of its route's line, or drops below Kirby's feet. Its end: within 196 pixels of the room's side it heads for it holds still until Kirby comes within 276 pixels of that side (on the DS: the stage's camera locking on its end arena), then loops: five quadratic Bezier legs from where it is through the loop's points (90, 60, 30, 30, 30 frames), off at a quarter of the last leg's end speed till it's out of the camera's view, and the loop again, for as long as Kirby's in the room. Squeak Squad's route is its stage's own (the map's points and zones); KatAM's rooms have none, so the corridor's (a8s4s3's) is laid on the room: its line 8 pixels under the floor it first bounces on (Squeak Squad's: 8 under the corridor's floor), its end point 68 pixels in from the room's side and 20 over that line (the stage's), the loop's points from there as the stage's. Checked against the DS (both stages loaded in the emulator, Kirby walked and floated along by a script: its spots, speeds and states every frame) and in KatAM (USA, room 0x323: the start, the hops and their waits, the end and its loop both ways, Kirby inhaling and touching it: nothing; a hop's speeds frame by frame as the DS's). Its sounds: Squeak Squad's (`SqueakSfx`). In Squeak Squad's own converted rooms (8-4-3, 8-4-4) it goes by the room's route as the DS does (`gSqueakDarkStarRoutes`, written by `KatAMExpansionSqueakRooms.cs`'s `SqueakDarkStarRoute()`: the zone per tile and the points, 8 bytes each as agbcc pads them): `RouteHop()` toward its zone's next point, backward at Kirby's speed + 0.88 or forward at his + 0.2 where the route turns back, waiting while he's more than 64 behind or 16 below; its end within 128 of the end point, its loop once Kirby's camera is held by the room's camera area; its spot the record's own. Replayed against the DS's recordings (Kirby forced to them each frame): the corridor close and its end and loop on the same frames; the shaft to the same end, off the DS's path for a while half-way. Elsewhere, the corridor's route laid on the room as above. |
| Gold Waddle Dee | enemy 0x0E | `squeak/gold_waddle_dee.c` | Walks at 1.25 (turning at walls), and per its variant (the template's `unk12`, Squeak Squad's spawn byte) patrols 24 pixels either side, jumps once at Kirby within 48 pixels, jumps every 121 frames, or stands; off a ledge it stops 5 frames in the air ("whoa") and falls; swims in water (1.2 across, sinking at 0.9) and jumps out. HP 6 (Squeak Squad's 30, three Waddle Dees'). Checked against the DS game (walk speed and animation, wall turn every other frame, jump arc, ledge hover, landing frame, the dust puff every 20 steps); its sounds are Squeak Squad's (`SqueakSfx`). |
